Let's refine optimization method.

Fast score function:
```
def fast_score(p):
    c=np.convolve(p,p)
    return float(2*len(p)*c.max())  # assuming sum=1
```
But if p sum not exactly 1 due to numeric, use sum^2: `2*n*c.max()/(p.sum()**2)`. To be safe.

Gradient/loss:
```
def gradient_soft_max(p, tau):
    c=np.convolve(p,p)
    max_c=c.max()
    if tau<=0: tau = max(1e-300, max_c*0.05)
    z = (c - max_c)/tau
    # if z large, exp underflow okay; but subtract max => <=0
    w=np.exp(z)
    s=w.sum()
    if s==0: w[c == max_c] = 1; w[c==max_c]/=...
    else: w/=s
    R=np.correlate(w, p, mode='full')[len(p)-1:2*len(p)-1]
    return R, max_c
```
Check correlate orientation: We need H_j=Σ_s w_s p_{s-j}. np.correlate(w,p,'full') index len(p)-1 + f? With len(w)=2n-1, len(p)=n. Earlier formula c_k=Σ_n a_{n+k} v_n. Output index len(v)-1+k. For k=j, index n-1+j. v=p, a=w. Yes H_j. Good.

Update:
```
def mirror_update(p, R, step):
    r_mean = R.mean()
    if r_mean <=0: return p
    r = R / r_mean
    # Clip r for stability
    r = np.clip(r, 0.0, 10.0)
    # exp step maybe
    factor = np.exp(-step * (r - 1.0))
    new = p * factor
    # enforce minimum
    new = np.maximum(new, 1e-15)
    new /= new.sum()
    return new
```
If step too large and r high, factor very small, zeros; clip introduces noise. Use step ≤1. Maybe use r = 1 + step * (r -1) (linear multiplicative) for stability: new = p * np.clip(1 - eta*(r-1)/max? , floor, inf). But exponential okay with small step.

We can add random Gaussian in log: `new *= exp(noise*sigma)` to avoid stuck. But too random may worsen. Use simulated annealing.

- If max_c==0 (uniform zeros?) set tau small.

- Compute score_new = fast_score(p_new). We already need c_next for next iteration. We can integrate: after update, compute c_new and score. If score_new < current_score - improve_tol, accept. def fft_convolve(a,b):
```
    n=a.size; m=b.size
    size = 1 << (n+m-2).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(b,size)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb, size)[:n+m-1]
    return out.real
```

Let's implement:
```
def conv_full(a,b=None, use_fft_threshold=2048):
    if b is None: b=a
    n=len(a); m=len(b)
    if n*m <= 4_000_000: return np.convolve(a,b)
    size = next_fast_len(n+m-1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b,size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,size)
    return out[:n+m-1]
```
Need import `scipy.fft.next_fast_len` or implement manual power of 2? FFT works with any size but power of 2/3 faster. `scipy` allowed but may not be installed? It is allowed.
```
def _next_pow2(L): return 1 << (L-1).bit_length()
```

Update using top active indices:
```
def active_gradient(p, c, topk=None, tau=None, use_top=True):
    n=len(p)
    max_c=c.max()
    if topk is None: topk = min(32, n*2-1)
    if topk >= len(c): idx = np.arange(len(c)); weights = ...
    else:
        # use topk largest values. Use argpartition; if tie okay.
        part = np.argpartition(c, -topk)[-topk:]
        # sort descending maybe for stable weights
        idx = part[np.argsort(c[part])[::-1]]
        vals = c[idx]
        if use softmax over top:
            vals_norm = vals - vals.max()
            tau_local = max(1e-12, (vals.max() - vals.min()) if vals.max()>vals.min() else vals.max()*0.1) * tau_frac? 
            w = np.exp(vals_norm/tau_local); normalize
        else: w = np.ones(topk)/topk
    R = np.zeros(n)
    for s, w in zip(idx, w):
        if w <=0: continue
        r_min = max(0, s-(n-1))
        r_max = min(n-1, s)
        if r_min <= r_max:
            r = np.arange(r_min, r_max+1)
            j = s - r
            R[j] += w * p[r]
    # scale R by 1/mean if mean >0
    return R
```
Wait if using topK softmax with vals normalized, weights sum 1. R magnitude ~1/n. If uniform topK, each weight 1/K, R = (1/K) sum over K shifts of p segment. Good.

Pseudo:
```
def optimize_sequence(p0, deadline, rng, topk=32, initial_step=0.25, floor=1e-10, use_fft=True, random_mix_every=50):
    p = normalize(np.asarray(p0,float))
    n=p.size
    c = conv_full(p,p,use_fft=use_fft)
    score = conv_score(c,p,n)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score
    step=initial_step
    rejects=0
    i=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        # perturb if rejects high
        if rejects > 30:
             # random multiplicative perturbation
             sig = min(0.5, 0.1 + rejects*0.001)
             p = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sig,size=n).astype(np.float64))
             p = floor_normalize(p,floor)
             c = conv_full(p,p); score=...
             if score < best_score: best...
             rejects=0
             continue
        R = active_gradient(p,c,topk,topk_tau=0.25)
        if not finite or mean=0: break
        cand = mirror_update(p,R,step,floor)
        c_new=conv_full(cand,cand); score_new=...
        if score_new < score - 1e-12 or score_new < best_score*(1-1e-6): accept?
        if score_new < score:
             p,c,score = cand,c_new,score_new
             if score < best_score: best update
             step = min(1.2, step*1.03)
             rejects=0
        else:
             # try half step
             cand2 = mirror_update(p,R,step*0.5,floor)
             c2=conv_full(cand2,cand2); score2=...
             if score2 < score:
                 p,c,score = cand2,c2,score2
                 if score<best: update
                 step = max(0.02, step*0.8)
             else:
                 # try random acceptance if close
                 if score_new < best_score*(1+0.002) and rng.random()<0.1:
                     p,c,score = cand,c_new,score_new
                     best maybe not
                 else:
                     step = max(0.01, step*0.7)
                     rejects += 1
        i+=1
        # periodically add small random smoothing to avoid local
        if i % 100 == 0:
             # mix with smoothed p? maybe p = 0.95p+0.05 random? 
    return best_p, best_score
```
Need be careful: If we reject and keep p, c remains current; good. If random perturbation changes p, recompute c.

- topk maybe min(32, 2*n-1). But for n small, topk maybe 8.
- We can use weights based on excess over a threshold: top K values, weight = vals - median_top if positive, normalized; if all equal, uniform. This focuses on the very max but also second. - Also include background uniform weight `eps` to avoid increasing unmonitored constraints? Could add `R += eps * p_like`? `floor_normalize(p,floor)`:
```
p = np.maximum(p, floor)
s=p.sum()
if s<=0: p=np.ones_like(p)/n
else: p=p/s
return p
```
If floor too large for small n, distorts. Use floor = min(1e-8, 1e-4/n?) Actually if n=10000, floor 1e-8 sum floor 1e-4, negligible. If n=64, floor 1e-8 sum 6e-7, okay. Use 1e-9.

But multiplicative update with floor can increase all near-zero entries every iteration? If p*factor underflows, set to floor. This introduces many tiny values; okay. Could slow due to all positive, but fine.

Random smooth initial generator:
```
def random_smooth_profile(n, rng):
    # Combine a low-frequency base and noise
    x = np.linspace(0,1,n)
    f = np.ones(n)
    # random amplitudes for modes 1..K
    K = max(2, int(np.sqrt(n))//2?)
    for k in range(1, K+1):
        amp = rng.uniform(0, 1.0) / (k**1.2) * 0.5
        phase = rng.uniform(0, 2*np.pi)
        f += amp * np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x + phase)
    # maybe add small Gaussian noise
    f += rng.normal(0,0.05,n)
    f = np.clip(f, 1e-4, None)
    return f/f.sum()
```
Need ensure nonnegative; clip and normalize.

Gaussian mixture profile:
```
def gaussian_mixture_profile(n, rng, n_components=3):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    f=np.zeros(n)
    for _ in range(n_components):
        c=rng.uniform(0,1); w=rng.uniform(0.05,0.4); a=rng.uniform(0.2,1.0)
        f += a*np.exp(-((x-c)**2)/(2*w*w))
    f += 1e-3
    f = np.clip(f,0,None)
    return f/f.sum()
```
Could include endpoint bias.

Ramp profile: `p= np.linspace(0.01,1,n)`, normalize. Also exponential decay.

Basis profile evaluation details:
```
def basis_make_profile(params, ctx):
    n,x,B = ctx
    f = 1.0 + params @ B  # B shape n,m
    # ensure positive: if min f < 0.2, scale? Use f = np.maximum(f, 0.05)
    f = np.clip(f, 0.05, None)
    s=f.sum(); if s<=0: f=np.ones(n)
    return f/s
```
B rows: For k=1..K: cos(2π k x)/k? If divide by k, amplitudes less constrained; use no divide but bounds small. Let's precompute B with orthonormal Fourier? Constant separate. For non-sym, phases. Use amplitude variable max 1. To avoid clipping, choose amplitude range ±0.8.

```
def make_basis_matrix(n, K):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    P0=np.ones(n)
    P1=2*x-1
    B=[P1]
    for k in 2..K:
       Pk = ((2k-1)x? shifted? Need shifted Legendre on [0,1]. Use recurrence with t=2x-1: Pk((t+1)/2). Recurrence: P_{k+1}=2k t P_k - (k-1)P_{k-1}? Standard Legendre on [-1,1]. Use t=2x-1.
       column Pk / (k+1?)
```

Coordinate search algorithm:
```
def optimize_basis_profile(n,K,deadline,rng,initial_params=None,verbose=False):
   x = np.linspace(0,1,n)
   B = legendre_basis(n,K)
   ctx=(n,x,B)
   if initial_params is None: params = rng.uniform(-0.7,0.7,K)
   else: params=initial_params.copy()
   best_params=params.copy(); best_p=make...; best_score=score
   step=0.1; stall=0
   while time < deadline:
      # pick strategy: coordinate perturbation, small random vector, restart
      if stall > 50 or rng.random()<0.01:
          # restart around best with noise
          params = best_params + rng.normal(0,0.2,size=K)
      else:
          idx = rng.integers(K)
          params = params.copy()
          params[idx] += rng.normal(0, step)
      p=make; s=score
      if s < best_score*(1-1e-5): update; stall=0; step=min(0.3, step*1.05)
      else:
          if s < best_score*1.02 and rng.random()<0.05: accept? For coordinate search maybe not.
          stall++; step=max(0.02, step*0.9)
   return best_params,best_p,best_score
```

Let's design top-level helper `legendre_basis(n, K)` returning B shape (K,n):
```
def legendre_basis(n,K):
    x = np.linspace(0,1,n)
    t = 2*x - 1
    P0=np.ones(n); P1=t
    cols=[P1]
    for k in range(1,K):
       # P_{k+1} = ((2k+1)t P_k - k P_{k-1})/(k+1)
       P0,P1 = P1, ((2*k+1)*t*P1 - k*P0)/(k+1)
       cols.append(P1)
    B=np.vstack(cols)
    # scale columns by 1/(k+1) maybe to keep amplitudes comparable
    for i in range(K):
        B[i] /= (i+1)
    return B
```
Check recurrence indices: Start k=1 to compute P2 from P1,P0. Standard: P_{n+1} = ((2n+1)t P_n - n P_{n-1})/(n+1). For n=1: P2=(3tP1 -1P0)/2 good.
We include P1...PK, no P0 constant. Good.
Scaling by i+1 reduces amplitude.

Maybe add basis columns for endpoint exponentials:
```
def extra_basis(n,K): # not necessary
```
Legendre covers.

`active_gradient_from_top(p, c, topk)`:
```
def active_gradient_from_top(p, c, topk, tau_weight=0.5):
    n=p.size
    m=c.size
    K = min(topk, m)
    if K == m:
       idx = np.arange(m)
       vals = c.copy()
    else:
       part = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
       idx = part[np.argsort(c[part])[::-1]]
       vals = c[idx]
    # weights: emphasize largest
    vspan = vals.max() - vals.min()
    if vspan <= 1e-30:
       w = np.ones(K)/K
    else:
       # temperature proportional to range * tau_weight
       t = max(vspan * tau_weight, 1e-30)
       w = np.exp((vals - vals.max())/t)
       sw = w.sum()
       if sw <= 0: w=...
       else: w /= sw
    R = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for s, ws in zip(idx, w):
        if ws <= 0: continue
        rmin = max(0, s - (n-1))
        rmax = min(n-1, s)
        if rmin <= rmax:
            # j = s - r
            R[s-rmax : s-rmin+1] ? Need assign reversed p segment.
            # r from rmin to rmax, j from s-rmin down to s-rmax? Let's do:
            r = np.arange(rmin, rmax+1)
            j = s - r
            R[j] += ws * p[r]
    return R
```
Using arange inside loop allocates; topk small, okay. Can optimize with slicing:
For r from rmin to rmax, j from s-rmin down to s-rmax descending. So segment R[s-rmin : s-rmax-1:-1]? Need careful. Simpler arange.
If topk=32, n=4096, loop 32*arrays length up to4096? Actually each r segment length up to n near center, total ~K*n=131k elements; allocating K arrays of length maybe average n/2, ~65k, okay. But Python overhead. Could use slicing with reversed view:
`R[s-rmin : s-rmax-1 : -1] += ws * p[rmin:rmax+1][::-1]`? Let's derive: rmin smallest r -> j=s-rmin largest. rmax largest -> j=s-rmax smallest. j sequence descending. In numpy slice `R[s-rmin : s-rmax-1 : -1]` goes from high to low inclusive high down to low. It matches p[rmin:rmax+1] in increasing r? The first high j corresponds rmin, so should use p[rmin] first. But vector addition with slice descending expects array in same order as slice (descending positions). If we provide p[rmin:rmax+1] (rmin to rmax), it aligns: slice position [s-rmin] gets p[rmin], next [s-rmin-1] gets p[rmin+1], good. So `R[s-rmin : s-rmax-1 : -1] += ws * p[rmin:rmax+1]`? Wait p[rmin:rmax+1] is increasing, slice descending, numpy aligns first element to first slice index (high). Good. Need stop index exclusive. For descending slice, stop should be s-rmax-1. Example n=5,s=3,rmin=0,rmax=3. j=3,2,1,0. Slice `R[3 : -1 : -1]` gives indices 3,2,1,0. p[0:4] aligns. Good. Use this for speed.
```
start = s - rmin
stop = s - rmax - 1
R[start:stop:-1] += ws * p[rmin:rmax+1]
```

`mirror_update`:
```
def mirror_update(p, R, step, floor=1e-9):
    Rmean = R.mean()
    if Rmean <= 0: return p.copy()
    r = R / Rmean
    # limit amplification
    r = np.clip(r, 0.0, 20.0)
    # Use tanh for stability? factor=1-step*(r-1) maybe
    # If step up to 0.8 and r up to 20, negative; exponential underflows.
    logfactor = -step * (r - 1.0)
    # clip logfactor to [-50,50]
    logfactor = np.clip(logfactor, -50.0, 50.0)
    newp = p * np.exp(logfactor)
    return floor_normalize(newp, floor)
```
For r<1, logfactor positive, increases low R entries, good. If step large and r high, low. Maybe use `np.tanh` to limit: `r = 1 + np.tanh(2*(R/Rmean -1))`? That compresses extreme. But simple with clip.

`floor_normalize`:
```
def floor_normalize(p, floor=1e-9):
    p = np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.maximum(p, floor)
    s = p.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
       return np.ones_like(p)/p.size
    p = p/s
    # Avoid values >1000? p ~1/n safe.
    return p
```

`conv_full` implementation. Use direct for small n to avoid FFT error and overhead. Threshold maybe 2048? Numpy direct O(n^2) in C. For n=2048, 4M ops fast. FFT may be similar. Let's set threshold 4096? Direct for 4096: 16M ops, maybe 0.015s; FFT: rfft 65536 ~ few ms? FFT likely faster. But FFT uses power-of-two size 65536, memory 0.5MB, okay. Let's use FFT for n>1024 to speed large. Need ensure full conv length correct. Use real fft.
```
def conv_full(a, b=None):
   if b is None: b=a
   n=len(a); m=len(b)
   if n*m <= 2_000_000:
       out=np.convolve(a,b)
   else:
       size = 1
       L=n+m-1
       while size < L: size <<=1
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
       fb=np.fft.rfft(b,size)
       out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,size)[:L]
       # remove tiny negatives
       np.maximum(out, 0.0, out=int? can't in-place with out)
       out = np.where(out < 0, 0.0, out)
   return out
```
`np.maximum(out,0,out)` works? yes `out` as third. Use `np.clip(out,0,None,out)`? `np.maximum(out, 0.0, out=out)`. Good.

For `irfft`, if size power-of-two, output length size. Slicing. Potential tiny negative at edges. Max unaffected.

`score_from_c(c,p)`:
```
def score_from_c(c,p):
    s=p.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*p.size*float(c.max())/(s*s))
```
During search p normalized so s≈1.

`optimize_sequence` details with active gradient. Need pass maybe `topk_factor`.
```
def optimize_sequence(p0, deadline, rng, topk=32, step0=0.25, floor=1e-9, iters_target=None):
   p = floor_normalize(np.asarray(p0,dtype=np.float64), floor)
   n=p.size
   c = conv_full(p,p)
   score = score_from_c(c,p)
   best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score
   step=step0
   rejects=0
   no_improve=0
   i=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       # If current not best, maybe occasionally reset to best and perturb
       if no_improve > 100:
           # random perturbation from best
           sig = min(0.4, 0.05 + 0.002*no_improve)
           p = best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sig, n))
           p = floor_normalize(p, floor)
           c = conv_full(p,p); score=score_from_c(c,p)
           if score < best_score: update
           no_improve=0
           step = max(0.1, step*0.5)
           i+=1
           continue
       R = active_gradient_from_top(p, c, topk)
       if not np.any(R>0): break
       cand = mirror_update(p,R,step,floor)
       c2 = conv_full(cand,cand)
       s2 = score_from_c(c2,cand)
       if s2 < score:
           p,c,score = cand,c2,s2
           step = min(1.0, step*1.05)
           rejects=0
           if score < best_score:
               best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score; no_improve=0
           else:
               no_improve +=1
       else:
           # try half step
           cand2 = mirror_update(p,R,step*0.5,floor)
           c3 = conv_full(cand2,cand2)
           s3=score_from_c(c3,cand2)
           if s3 < score:
               p,c,score = cand2,c3,s3
               step = max(0.02, step*0.8)
               rejects=0
               if score < best_score: update
               else: no_improve+=1
           else:
               # random acceptance if close
               if s2 < best_score*(1+0.005) and rng.random() < 0.05:
                   p,c,score = cand,c2,s2
               step = max(0.02, step*0.7)
               rejects +=1
               no_improve +=1
       i+=1
       # topk adapt: if no_improve high, use larger topk? 
   return best_p,best_score
```
If s2 < score but not <best, no_improve? It is an improvement over current but not global best; we can reset no_improve maybe? Use `if score < best_score` only.
```
def block_perturb(p, rng, nblocks):
   cand=p.copy()
   for _ in range(nblocks):
       width = rng.integers(max(1,n//100), max(2,n//10))
       start = rng.integers(0,n-width)
       cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.2))
   return floor_normalize(cand, floor)
```
This is cheap O(nblocks*width) but allocate. For n=8192, random noise full O(n) okay. Use log-noise full when stuck.

```
def _basis_objective(params):
```

`basis_make_profile(params, n, B)` top-level.
```
def basis_make_profile(params, n, B):
    f = 1.0 + params @ B
    f = np.clip(f, 0.02, None)
    s=f.sum()
    if s<=0: f=np.ones(n)
    else: f=f/s
    return f
```

Coordinate search:
```
def optimize_basis_profile(n,K,deadline,rng,bound=1.0,initial=None):
    B=legendre_basis(n,K)
    if initial is None: params = rng.uniform(-0.5*bound,0.5*bound,K)
    else: params=initial.copy()
    params = np.clip(params, -bound, bound)
    p=make; score=...
    best...

    while time<deadline:
        if stall > 40 or rng.random()<0.02:
            params = best_params + rng.normal(0,0.25,K)
            params[stall%K] += rng.normal(0,step)??
        else:
            params = best_params.copy()
            idx = rng.integers(K)
            params[idx] += rng.normal(0,step)
            params[idx] = clip
        p=make; s=score
        if s<best: update, step=min(0.4,step*1.05), stall=0
        else:
            # if close, accept maybe
            if s < best*1.01 and rng.random()<0.1:
                best? no, but current? For coordinate search, we can keep as current to chain.
            stall++; step=max(0.03,step*0.9)
    return best_params,best_p,best_score
```

Define `soft_loss_and_grad_u(u, tau)`:
- p = softmax(u) (exp(u - max)/sum)
- c=conv(p,p)
- max_c=c.max()
- L = max_c + tau * logsumexp((c - max_c)/tau) (smooth max)
- weights w = exp((c-max_c)/tau)/sum
- G = correlate(w,p,full)[n-1:2n-1] (∂max_loss? for logsumexp part; plus ∂max? The smooth max gradient 2*correlate? We can use pG - sum pG). Since scale invariant and sum fixed, gradient wrt u: p*G - p*mean? Actually if L( p_u ) smooth max, ∂L/∂u_j = ∑_i ∂L/∂p_i ∂p_i/∂u_j. p=softmax, ∂p_i/∂u_j = p_i(δ_{ij} - p_j). So ∂L/∂u_j = p_j G_j - (∑ p_i G_i) p_j = p_j (G_j - hmean), where hmean = ∑ p_i G_i /? Since ∑ p_i G_i is scalar. So gradient = p*(G - weighted_mean). G we computed without factor 2; constant. Good.
Objective can just be `score`? For L-BFGS, smooth. We can minimize `smooth_max_score = 2n*(max_c + tau*logsumexp((c-max_c)/tau))` maybe divided by sum? p sum1. Good.

`optimize_basis_search`:
```
def optimize_basis_search(n, K, deadline, rng):
    B = legendre_basis(n,K)
    best_p = None; best_score=np.inf
    # try DE if available
    if HAVE_DE and n <= 512:
       global _BASIS_CTX
       _BASIS_CTX=(n,B)
       bounds=[(-1.0,1.0)]*K
       for attempt in range(max(1, int((deadline - time.monotonic())//5)+1)):
           maxiter = 60 if n>128 else 100
           try:
               res = differential_evolution(_basis_objective, bounds, seed=int(rng.integers(0,2**31)), maxiter=maxiter, popsize=10, tol=1e-4, polish=False, workers=1)
           except Exception:
               break
           if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
           p = basis_make_profile(res.x,n,B)
           c=conv_full(p,p); s=score_from_c(c,p)
           if s<best_score: best... params=res.x
    # coordinate search for remaining
    if best_params is None: initial = rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5,K)
    else: initial=best_params
    p,score = ...
    step=0.1; stall=0
    while time<deadline:
        ... top-level coordinate as above
    return best_params,best_p,best_score
```

```
def upsample_profile(p, n_to):
   m=len(p)
   if m==n_to: return p.copy()
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n_to)
   np.interp(x_new, x_old, p, out? cannot out for interp? use np.interp returns)
   return floor_normalize(interp,1e-12)
```

`random_smooth_profile(n,rng)`:
```
def random_smooth_profile(n, rng):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   f=np.ones(n)
   K=max(3, int(math.sqrt(n))//2)
   for k in range(1,K+1):
       amp = rng.uniform(0.0, 0.8) / math.sqrt(k)
       phase = rng.uniform(0, 2*math.pi)
       f += amp*np.cos(2*math.pi*k*x+phase)
   # add endpoint ramp option
   if rng.random()<0.5:
       f += rng.uniform(0.1,0.8)*(x-0.5)
   f += rng.normal(0,0.03,n)
   f=np.clip(f,0.02,None)
   return f/f.sum()
```
Maybe amplitude too high causing clipping; okay.

`block_profile` maybe:
```
def structured_block_profile(n,rng):
   k = rng.integers(3,7)
   p=np.zeros(n)
   # allocate lengths
   lengths = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(k)*2) * n
   start=0
   for L in lengths:
      L=int(max(2,L))
      if L>n: L=n
      h=rng.uniform(0.2,2.0)
      p[start:start+L]=h
      start += L + max(1, int(n*(0.02 + 0.05*rng.random()))) # gap
      if start>n: break
   if p.sum()<=0: p=ones
   return p/p.sum()
```
Could be a random init. Add to medium profiles.

```
def run_phase(n, inits, slice_seconds, rng, candidates):
```

```
    else:
        params=None; p_b=None; s_b=np.inf
    # Determine phase1 length/inits

    if len(inits1)>4: inits1=inits1[:4]
    phase_end = min(now + slice1, deadline - 30)
    run_phase(n1, inits1, phase_list? we need use slice? Let's call run_phase(n1,inits1, phase_end-now, deadline, rng,candidates)
```

```
def run_phase(n, inits, phase_end, total_deadline, rng, candidates, topk=None, step0=None):
    phase_start=now
    best_p=None; best_score=np.inf
    per=(phase_end-phase_start)/len(inits)
    for idx,p0 in enumerate(inits):
        seg_end=min(phase_start+per*(idx+1), total_deadline)
        p,s=optimize_sequence(p0,seg_end,rng,topk=topk,step0=step0)
        add_candidate(candidates,p,s)
        if s<best_score: best_p=p; best_score=s
    return best_p,best_score
```

```
def random_lognoise_update(p,rng,sigma=0.1):
    noise = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, p.size)
    return floor_normalize(p * np.exp(noise), 1e-9)
```

`block_perturb`:
```
def block_perturb(p,rng, nblocks=3, sigma=0.2):
   cand=p.copy()
   n=cand.size
   for _ in range(nblocks):
      width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//200), max(2,n//20)))
      start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width))
      cand[start:start+width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma)))
   return floor_normalize(cand,1e-9)
```
Use when stuck.

Let's draft code more concretely.

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
try:
    from scipy.optimize import differential_evolution
    _HAVE_DE = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_DE = False

_BASIS_CTX = None

def legendre_basis(n, K):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 2.0*x - 1.0
    p0 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p1 = t.copy()
    cols = [p1 / 1.0]
    for k in range(1, K):
        # compute P_{k+1}
        p2 = ((2.0*k + 1.0)*t*p1 - k*p0) / (k + 1.0)
        p0, p1 = p1, p2
        cols.append(p1 / (k + 1.0))
    if len(cols) < K:
        # if K=0? not
        pass
    B = np.vstack(cols[:K])
    return B
```
If K=0, but we call K>=4. Good.

Basis objective:
```
def _basis_objective(params):
    ctx = _BASIS_CTX
    if ctx is None: return np.inf
    n, B = ctx
    f = 1.0 + params @ B
    f = np.clip(f, 0.02, None)
    s = f.sum()
    if s <= 0: return np.inf
    f = f / s
    c = conv_full(f, f)
    return float(2.0 * n * c.max())
```
Since p sum1. Use conv_full; for n=96 direct.

`basis_make_profile`:
```
def basis_make_profile(params, n, B):
    f=1.0 + params @ B
    f=np.clip(f,0.02,None)
    s=f.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones(n)/n
    f=f/s
    return f
```
`make_profile_from_params`:
```
def make_profile_from_params(params,n):
    params=np.asarray(params,dtype=np.float64)
    K=int(params.size)
    if K==0: return np.ones(n)/n
    B=legendre_basis(n,K)
    return basis_make_profile(params,n,B)
```

`optimize_basis_search` coordinate after DE:
```
def optimize_basis_search(n,K,deadline,rng):
    B=legendre_basis(n,K)
    best_params=None; best_p=None; best_score=np.inf
    if _HAVE_DE:
       global _BASIS_CTX
       _BASIS_CTX=(n,B)
       bounds=[(-1.2,1.2)]*K
       # DE attempts with time budget maybe
       de_start=time.monotonic()
       max_total_de = min(max(5.0, (deadline-de_start)*0.5), 60.0)
       for attempt in range(3):
          if time.monotonic() > de_start + max_total_de: break
          try:
             maxiter = 50 if n>128 else 80
             res = differential_evolution(_basis_objective, bounds, seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31)), maxiter=maxiter, popsize=8, tol=1e-4, polish=False)
             params=np.clip(res.x,-1.2,1.2)
             p=basis_make_profile(params,n,B)
             s=score_from_c(conv_full(p,p),p)
             if s<best_score:
                 best_score=s; best_p=p.copy(); best_params=params.copy()
          except Exception:
             break
       _BASIS_CTX=None
    if best_params is None:
       best_params = rng.uniform(-0.6,0.6,K)
    current_params = best_params.copy()
    current_p = basis_make_profile(current_params,n,B)
    current_score = score_from_c(conv_full(current_p,current_p),current_p)
    if current_score < best_score: ...
    step=0.12; stall=0; restart_count=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if stall > 35 or (rng.random() < 0.01):
           current_params = best_params + rng.normal(0.0, 0.3, K)
           current_params = np.clip(current_params, -1.2, 1.2)
           stall=0
       else:
           current_params = best_params.copy()  # maybe chain from best, not current? Use best to avoid drift.
           idx=rng.integers(0,K)
           current_params[idx] += rng.normal(0.0, step)
           current_params=np.clip(current_params,-1.2,1.2)
       p=basis_make_profile(current_params,n,B)
       s=score_from_c(conv_full(p,p),p)
       if s < best_score:
          best...; current best; step=min(0.5,step*1.05); stall=0
       else:
          # if close, accept as current? Since we restart from best, current_params maybe not stored. To chain, set best_params? no.
          if s < best_score*(1.0+0.005) and rng.random()<0.1:
              # allow a temporary current? We use best_params for next, so can't chain. Maybe if close, update current base?
              pass
          stall +=1; step=max(0.02,step*0.9)
    return best_params,best_p,best_score
```
If we always perturb from best_params, it's local hill climb with restarts. Good enough. It doesn't chain multiple small improvements from non-best unless they become best. That's okay.

Maybe use DE polish? no.

Now, `optimize_sequence` code refine:
```
def optimize_sequence(p0, deadline, rng, topk=None, step0=0.25, floor=1e-9):
    p=floor_normalize(np.asarray(p0,dtype=np.float64), floor)
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return None,np.inf
    c=conv_full(p,p)
    score=score_from_c(c,p)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score
    if topk is None:
        topk = min(32, max(8, n//16))
    step=step0
    no_best=0
    no_improve=0
    i=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if no_best > 150:
            # reset to best and block perturb
            p = block_perturb(best_p, rng, int(rng.integers(2,5)), float(0.15 + 0.05*rng.random()))
            c = conv_full(p,p); score=score_from_c(c,p)
            if score < best_score:
                best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score; no_best=0
            no_improve=0
            step=max(0.1, step*0.5)
            i+=1
            continue
        if no_improve > 60:
            # mild log noise
            p = random_lognoise_update(p, rng, 0.08)
            c=conv_full(p,p); score=score_from_c(c,p)
            if score < best_score: best...
            no_improve=0
            i+=1
            continue
        R=active_gradient_from_top(p,c,topk)
        if not np.any(R>0.0):
            break
        cand=mirror_update(p,R,step,floor)
        c2=conv_full(cand,cand); s2=score_from_c(c2,cand)
        if s2 < score:
            p,c,score=cand,c2,s2
            no_improve=0
            step=min(1.0, step*1.03)
            if score < best_score:
                best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score; no_best=0
            else:
                no_best +=1
        else:
            cand2=mirror_update(p,R,step*0.5,floor)
            c3=conv_full(cand2,cand2); s3=score_from_c(c3,cand2)
            if s3 < score:
                p,c,score=cand2,c3,s3
                no_improve=0
                step=max(0.02, step*0.85)
                if score < best_score:
                    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score; no_best=0
                else:
                    no_best +=1
            else:
                # accept close with small probability
                if s2 < best_score*(1.0+0.003) and rng.random() < 0.1:
                    p,c,score=cand,c2,s2
                step=max(0.02, step*0.7)
                no_improve +=1
                no_best +=1
        i+=1
    return best_p,best_score
```
`no_best` counts iterations since best improved. If best not improve but current improves? current can't improve below best? It can improve from a worse current to still above best; no_best increments. If no_best >150, reset to best. If current still close to best maybe okay. `no_improve` counts consecutive rejections. Good.

`random_lognoise_update` top-level:
```
def random_lognoise_update(p, rng, sigma):
    p = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma,p.size))
    return floor_normalize(p, 1e-9)
```

`block_perturb`:
```
def block_perturb(p, rng, nblocks, sigma):
    cand=p.copy()
    n=cand.size
    for _ in range(nblocks):
        low=min(n//200, n)
        if low<1: low=1
        high=max(low+1, n//20)
        width=int(rng.integers(low, high))
        width=min(width,n)
        start=int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
        cand[start:start+width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
    return floor_normalize(cand, 1e-9)
```
If n small, high issues; ensure.

`floor_normalize` floor param maybe too high for n small; 1e-9 okay.

`active_gradient_from_top` topk maybe 32. Weights softmax over top K with temperature `tau_weight=0.25` times span. If top values very close, uniform.
```
def active_gradient_from_top(p, c, topk):
    n=p.size; m=c.size
    K=min(topk,m)
    if K <=0: return np.zeros(n)
    if K == m:
       idx=np.arange(m); vals=c
    else:
       part=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
       idx=part[np.argsort(c[part])[::-1]]
       vals=c[idx]
    vmax=float(vals.max())
    vmin=float(vals.min())
    if vmax <= 0:
       w=np.ones(K)/K
    elif vmax - vmin < 1e-15:
       w=np.ones(K)/K
    else:
       temp=(vmax-vmin)*0.25
       w=np.exp((vals-vmax)/temp)
       sw=w.sum()
       if sw<=0: w=np.ones(K)/K
       else: w=w/sw
    R=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
    for s, ws in zip(idx, w):
        if ws <= 1e-300: continue
        s=int(s)
        rmin = max(0, s-(n-1))
        rmax = min(n-1, s)
        if rmin <= rmax:
            start = s - rmin
            stop = s - rmax - 1
            R[start:stop:-1] += ws * p[rmin:rmax+1]
    return R
```
Check if `start:stop:-1` when start==0? stop would be -rmax-1? If start 0, descending slice from 0 to? not happen because start=s-rmin. `mirror_update` with R maybe contains NaN? check.
```
def mirror_update(p,R,step,floor=1e-9):
    rmean=float(R.mean())
    if not np.isfinite(rmean) or rmean <= 0:
         return p.copy()
    r=R/rmean
    r=np.nan_to_num(r, nan=0.0, posinf=20.0, neginf=0.0)
    r=np.clip(r,0.0,20.0)
    logf = -step*(r-1.0)
    logf=np.clip(logf,-50.0,50.0)
    newp = p * np.exp(logf)
    return floor_normalize(newp, floor)
```
If step=1 and r=20, logf=-19, factor 5e-9, okay. If r=0, logf=1, factor e. Good.

```
def self_fast_score(p):
```

```
MAX_CAND_LEN = 10000
def add_candidate(candidates, p, score):
    if p is None: return None
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    if p.ndim !=1: p=p.ravel()
    if p.size ==0: return None
    if p.size > MAX_CAND_LEN:
        p=upsample_profile(p, MAX_CAND_LEN)
    p=floor_normalize(p,1e-10)
    if not np.isfinite(score) or score <=0:
        score=self_fast_score(p)
    candidates.append((float(score), p.copy()))
    # If too many, prune worst by score
    if len(candidates) > 20:
        candidates.sort(key=candidate_key)
        del candidates[12:]  # keep top12? But del after sort loses original? okay.
    return p
```

`upsample_profile`:
```
def upsample_profile(p, n_to):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    m=p.size
    if m == n_to: return p.copy()
    if m ==1:
       arr=np.full(n_to, float(p[0]))
       return floor_normalize(arr,1e-10)
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_to)
    arr=np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    return floor_normalize(arr,1e-10)
```
If n_to much larger, okay.

`random_smooth_profile`:
```
def random_smooth_profile(n, rng):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   f=np.ones(n)
   K=max(3, int(math.sqrt(n))//2)
   for k in range(1,K+1):
       amp = rng.uniform(0.0, 0.8) / math.sqrt(float(k))
       phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*math.pi)
       f += amp*np.cos(2.0*math.pi*k*x + phase)
   if rng.random() < 0.7:
       f += rng.uniform(-0.8,0.8) * (x - 0.5)
   if rng.random() < 0.4:
       f += rng.uniform(0.0,0.6) * (1.0 - x)
   f += rng.normal(0.0,0.02,n)
   f=np.clip(f,0.02,None)
   s=f.sum()
   if s<=0: f=np.ones(n)
   else: f=f/s
   return f
```

`structured_block_profile` optional:
```
def structured_block_profile(n,rng):
   p=np.zeros(n)
   k=int(rng.integers(3,7))
   lengths=rng.dirichlet(np.full(k, 1.5)) * n
   start=0
   for L in lengths:
       L=max(2,int(L))
       gap=int(max(1, n*0.02*rng.random()))
       if start+L>n: L=n-start
       if L<=0: break
       h=rng.uniform(0.2,2.0)
       p[start:start+L]=h
       start += L + gap
       if start>n: break
   if p.sum()<=0:
       p=np.ones(n)
   else:
       p=p/p.sum()
   return p
```
Could be a random init but low smoothness; include in medium inits if time.

`run_phase`:
```
def run_phase(n, inits, phase_deadline, total_deadline, rng, candidates, topk=None, step0=None):
    phase_start=time.monotonic()
    if phase_deadline <= phase_start or len(inits)==0:
       return None, np.inf
    phase_deadline=min(phase_deadline, total_deadline)
    per=(phase_deadline-phase_start)/float(len(inits))
    best_p=None; best_score=np.inf
    for idx,p0 in enumerate(inits):
       seg_deadline=min(phase_start + per*(float(idx)+1.0), total_deadline)
       if seg_deadline <= time.monotonic():
           # no time, maybe just add initial
           s=self_fast_score(p0)
           add_candidate(candidates,p0,s)
           if s<best_score: best_p=p0.copy(); best_score=s
           continue
       p,s=optimize_sequence(p0, seg_deadline, rng, topk=topk, step0=step0)
       if p is not None:
           add_candidate(candidates,p,s)
       else:
           add_candidate(candidates,p0,self_fast_score(p0))
       if s < best_score:
           best_score=s; best_p=np.asarray(p0? wait p is returned, but p variable later reassigned. Store copy.)
           best_p=p.copy()
    print(f"phase n={n} best fast={best_score:.6f}")
    return best_p, best_score
```
Need if `p` None but initial added. In best update if p None, use p0.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=1500,**kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    budget=max(10.0,float(budget_s))
    reserve=max(15.0, 0.015*budget)
    total_deadline=t0+budget-reserve
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    candidates=[]
    add_candidate(candidates, np.ones(1024)/1024.0, 2.0)
    # print
    parent=None
    parent_obj=globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if parent_obj is not None:
       try: ...
       except: parent=None
       if parent is not None:
           ppar=upsample_profile(parent, min(4096,parent.size))
           add_candidate(candidates, ppar, None)
    if budget < 20:
       # finalize quickly
       ...
    # Basis if time
    params=None; p_basis=None
    if total_deadline - time.monotonic() > 10:
       if budget > 800: nb=128; K=10; basis_slice=30
       elif budget > 300: nb=96; K=8; basis_slice=15
       else: nb=64; K=6; basis_slice=min(10, total_deadline-t0)
       basis_deadline=min(time.monotonic()+basis_slice, total_deadline-5)
       params,p_basis,s_basis=optimize_basis_search(nb,K,basis_deadline,rng)
       if p_basis is not None: add_candidate(candidates,p_basis,s_basis)
       print('basis',s_basis)
    # choose phases
    now=time.monotonic()
    if budget > 800:
       n1=512; slice1=150
       n2=2048; slice2=300
       n3=8192
    elif budget > 300:
       n1=384; slice1=80
       n2=1024; slice2=180
       n3=4096
    else:
       n1=256; slice1=40
       n2=512; slice2=80
       n3=2048
    inits1=[]
    if params is not None: inits1.append(make_profile_from_params(params,n1))
    inits1.append(random_smooth_profile(n1,rng))
    if len(inits1)<3: inits1.append(structured_block_profile(n1,rng))
    if parent is not None: inits1.append(upsample_profile(parent,n1))
    if len(inits1)>4: inits1=inits1[:4]
    phase1_deadline=min(time.monotonic()+slice1, total_deadline)
    p1,s1=run_phase(n1,inits1,phase1_deadline,total_deadline,rng,candidates)
    # phase2
    inits2=[]
    if p1 is not None: inits2.append(p1)
    if params is not None: inits2.append(make_profile_from_params(params,n2))
    inits2.append(random_smooth_profile(n2,rng))
    if parent is not None: inits2.append(upsample_profile(parent,n2))
    if len(inits2)>3: inits2=inits2[:3]
    # Need ensure initial unique? not needed.
    if total_deadline - time.monotonic() > 5:
       phase2_deadline=min(time.monotonic()+slice2, total_deadline)
       p2,s2=run_phase(n2,inits2,phase2_deadline,total_deadline,rng,candidates)
    else: p2,p2score = p1,s1
    # phase3
    inits3=[]
    if p2 is not None: inits3.append(p2)
    if params is not None and n3 != n2: inits3.append(make_profile_from_params(params,n3))
    if parent is not None: inits3.append(upsample_profile(parent,n3))
    if len(inits3)>2: inits3=inits3[:2]
    if total_deadline - time.monotonic() > 10:
       phase3_deadline=total_deadline
       p3,s3=run_phase(n3,inits3,phase3_deadline,total_deadline,rng,candidates)
    # final exact
    candidates.sort(key=candidate_key)
    top=candidates[:8]
    best_seq=None; best_exact=np.inf
    for score,p in top:
       seq=[float(x) for x in p]
       try: exc=float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
       except Exception: exc=np.inf
       print('candidate len',len(p),'fast',score,'exact',exc)
       if exc < best_exact:
          best_exact=exc; best_seq=seq
    if best_seq is None:
       fallback=np.ones(1024)/1024.0
       best_seq=[float(x) for x in fallback]
    print('final best', best_exact)
    return best_seq
```

Another enhancement: The update should use `R = correlate(weights, p)` for weights over active constraints, but we multiply p by exp(-R). In actual gradient of soft loss, log-parameter gradient is `R_j - weighted_mean(R_j?)` maybe not R/Rmean -1; subtracting mean of R over j, not weighted by p. We used mean over j. Is that the correct natural gradient? For softmax parameterization, log update if we do p ∝ p exp(-η G) changes sum and not subtract mean; normalization effectively subtracts weighted mean of log factor, not mean over j. Our `r - 1` with mean over j enforces sum of log changes weighted? Actually product p_j exp(-η(r_j-1)); sum new approx old sum * (1 - η(mean_j r_j -1)) = old if mean over j of r_j=1. This keeps the unweighted sum of log changes zero, not the weighted sum by p_j. Natural gradient would subtract `Σ p_j r_j / Σ p_j` (weighted mean) but p normalized so mean p_j r_j = mean_{uniform}(p_j r_j) not r_j. The mirror descent on probability simplex for objective L(p) uses p_new ∝ p * exp(-η ∇L(p)), no subtract mean; normalization handles scale and preserves sum. It does not require subtracting unweighted mean. Subtracting unweighted mean may move differently, but because we then normalize, adding a constant to log update doesn't change p after normalization. Wait if we do p_j exp(-η(r_j - c)), normalization removes factor exp(η c), so constant c irrelevant. The normalization effectively subtracts weighted mean of log update, not our chosen c. Thus `r - 1` is equivalent to `r` up to normalization! Because normalization divides by sum p_j exp(-η r_j). So subtracting unweighted mean is unnecessary and can affect only before normalization? Actually p_j exp(-η(r_j -1)) normalized = p_j exp(-η r_j) / Σ p_j exp(-η(r_k -1)) = same as p_j exp(-η r_j)/Σ p_j exp(-η r_k). So the constant 1 cancels. Therefore using `r-1` vs `r` is same after normalization. Good. But we also clip r and floor before normalize; clipping constant? not identical but okay. It might reduce extreme effects? Since normalization cancels global shifts, clipping to [0,20] can matter. We clip before; if many zeros, mean maybe >? okay.

Maybe we should not clip r to 20; but to avoid overflow. Good.

The natural gradient update p ∝ p exp(-η G). If G=0 for many entries, normalization increases them proportionally. This is what we do.

Step size in natural gradient η scale? G~1/n, η step? We scale by mean, so r~O(1). Good.

Now, active top weights sum1; G magnitude ~1/n. Scaling by mean makes O(1). Good.

```python
"""
Search for low-value nonnegative step-function coefficients.

Strategy:
1. Find a good smooth low-dimensional profile using Legendre basis coefficients
   (differential evolution + stochastic coordinate polish).
2. Use that profile to initialize full-sequence optimizers at increasing lengths.
3. Full-sequence optimizer uses an active-constraint multiplicative mirror update:
   - compute full convolution,
   - take the most violated convolution coefficients,
   - form a weighted active gradient,
   - update p multiplicatively in log-space,
   - keep the best sequence found so far.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import differential_evolution
    _HAVE_DE = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_DE = False

_BASIS_CTX = None
_MAX_CAND_LEN = 10000


def legendre_basis(n, K):
    """Shifted Legendre basis P_1,...,P_K on [0,1], rows in an (K,n) matrix."""
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 2.0 * x - 1.0

    p0 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p1 = t.copy()
    cols = [p1 / 1.0]

    for k in range(1, K):
        # P_{k+1} = ((2k+1)t P_k - k P_{k-1}) / (k+1)
        p2 = ((2.0 * k + 1.0) * t * p1 - k * p0) / (k + 1.0)
        p0, p1 = p1, p2
        cols.append(p1 / (k + 1.0))

    return np.vstack(cols[:K])


def basis_make_profile(params, n, B):
    """Make a nonnegative probability vector from basis parameters."""
    params = np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64)
    f = 1.0 + params @ B
    f = np.nan_to_num(f, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    f = np.clip(f, 0.01, None)
    s = f.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    f = f / s
    return np.maximum(f, 0.0)


def make_profile_from_params(params, n):
    """Create a full length-n profile from optimized basis parameters."""
    params = np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64)
    K = int(params.size)
    if K <= 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    B = legendre_basis(n, K)
    return basis_make_profile(params, n, B)


def _basis_objective(params):
    """Objective for differential evolution, using a context global."""
    try:
        ctx = _BASIS_CTX
        if ctx is None:
            return np.inf
        n, B = ctx
        params = np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64)
        f = 1.0 + params @ B
        f = np.nan_to_num(f, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
        f = np.clip(f, 0.01, None)
        s = f.sum()
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            return np.inf
        f = f / s
        c = conv_full(f, f)
        return float(2.0 * n * float(c.max()))
    except Exception:
        return np.inf


def conv_full(a, b=None):
    """Full convolution of real arrays. Direct for small sizes, FFT for larger."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if b is None:
        b = a
        n = int(a.size)
        if n <= 0:
            return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
        L = 2 * n - 1
        if n * n <= 2_000_000:
            out = np.convolve(a, a).astype(np.float64, copy=False)
        else:
            size = 1
            while size < L:
                size <<= 1
            fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
            out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)[:L].astype(np.float64, copy=False)
            np.maximum(out, 0.0, out=out)
        return out

    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(b.size)
    if n <= 0 or m <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = n + m - 1
    if n * m <= 2_000_000:
        out = np.convolve(a, b).astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    else:
        size = 1
        while size < L:
            size <<= 1
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
        fb = np.fft.rfft(b, size)
        out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, size)[:L].astype(np.float64, copy=False)
        np.maximum(out, 0.0, out=out)
    return out


def score_from_c(c, p):
    """Score 2*n*max(c)/(sum(p)^2). The sequence p is assumed to produce c."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size > 0 else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * p.size * cmax / (s * s))


def self_fast_score(p):
    """Fast score of a normalized/nonnegative sequence."""
    p = floor_normalize(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64), 1.0e-12)
    c = conv_full(p, p)
    return score_from_c(c, p)


def floor_normalize(p, floor=1.0e-9):
    """Clip small negative/NaNs, enforce a tiny floor, and normalize to sum 1."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, float(floor))
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, p.size)
    p = p / s
    return p


def active_gradient_from_top(p, c, topk):
    """
    Weighted active gradient R_j = sum_s w_s p_{s-j} for the top violating
    convolution coefficients. This is the directional derivative used for
    multiplicative updates.
    """
    n = int(p.size)
    m = int(c.size)
    if n <= 0 or m <= 0:
        return np.zeros(max(0, n), dtype=np.float64)

    K = int(min(max(1, topk), m))

    if K == m:
        idx = np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
        vals = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        part = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
        idx = part[np.argsort(c[part])[::-1]]
        vals = c[idx].astype(np.float64, copy=False)

    vmax = float(vals.max())
    vmin = float(vals.min())
    if not np.isfinite(vmax) or vmax <= 0.0 or (vmax - vmin) <= 1.0e-18:
        w = np.full(K, 1.0 / K, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        temp = max(1.0e-30, 0.25 * (vmax - vmin))
        w = np.exp((vals - vmax) / temp)
        sw = float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
            w = np.full(K, 1.0 / K, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            w = w / sw

    R = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for s, ws in zip(idx, w):
        if ws <= 0.0:
            continue
        s = int(s)
        rmin = max(0, s - (n - 1))
        rmax = min(n - 1, s)
        if rmin <= rmax:
            start = s - rmin
            stop = s - rmax - 1
            R[start:stop:-1] += ws * p[rmin:rmax + 1]

    return R


def mirror_update(p, R, step, floor=1.0e-9):
    """
    Multiplicative update in probability simplex:
        p_new ~ p * exp(-step * R)
    Normalization removes additive constants in the log-update.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    R = np.asarray(R, dtype=np.float64)
    rmean = float(R.mean())
    if not np.isfinite(rmean) or rmean <= 0.0:
        return p.copy()

    r = R / rmean
    r = np.nan_to_num(r, nan=0.0, posinf=20.0, neginf=0.0)
    r = np.clip(r, 0.0, 20.0)

    logfactor = -float(step) * (r - 1.0)
    logfactor = np.clip(logfactor, -50.0, 50.0)
    newp = p * np.exp(logfactor)
    return floor_normalize(newp, floor)


def random_lognoise_update(p, rng, sigma):
    """Mild positive re-noising, preserving nonnegativity."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    noise = rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), p.size)
    return floor_normalize(p * np.exp(noise), 1.0e-9)


def block_perturb(p, rng, nblocks, sigma):
    """Random block multiplicative perturbation."""
    cand = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = int(cand.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return cand

    low = max(1, n // 200)
    high = max(low + 1, n // 20)
    for _ in range(max(1, int(nblocks))):
        width = int(rng.integers(low, high))
        width = int(min(max(1, width), n))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
        scale = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma))))
        cand[start:start + width] *= scale
    return floor_normalize(cand, 1.0e-9)


def optimize_basis_search(n, K, deadline, rng):
    """
    Optimize a low-dimensional Legendre profile.
    Returns (params, p, score).
    """
    B = legendre_basis(n, K)
    best_params = None
    best_p = None
    best_score = np.inf
    bound = 1.2

    if _HAVE_DE:
        global _BASIS_CTX
        _BASIS_CTX = (n, B)
        bounds = [(float(-bound), float(bound))] * K
        de_start = time.monotonic()
        de_budget = min(60.0, max(5.0, 0.6 * max(0.0, deadline - de_start)))

        for attempt in range(3):
            if time.monotonic() > de_start + de_budget:
                break
            try:
                maxiter = 70 if n <= 96 else 45
                res = differential_evolution(
                    _basis_objective,
                    bounds,
                    seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1)),
                    maxiter=maxiter,
                    popsize=8,
                    tol=1.0e-4,
                    polish=False,
                )
                params = np.clip(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64), -bound, bound)
                p = basis_make_profile(params, n, B)
                c = conv_full(p, p)
                s = score_from_c(c, p)
                if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
                    best_score = s
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    best_params = params.copy()
            except Exception:
                break
        _BASIS_CTX = None

    if best_params is None:
        best_params = rng.uniform(-0.6 * bound, 0.6 * bound, K)
    best_params = np.clip(best_params, -bound, bound)

    current_p = basis_make_profile(best_params, n, B)
    current_score = score_from_c(conv_full(current_p, current_p), current_p)
    if np.isfinite(current_score) and current_score < best_score:
        best_score = current_score
        best_p = current_p.copy()

    step = 0.14
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if stall > 35 or (rng.random() < 0.01):
            params = best_params + rng.normal(0.0, 0.28, K)
            params = np.clip(params, -bound, bound)
            stall = 0
        else:
            params = best_params.copy()
            idx = int(rng.integers(0, K))
            params[idx] += rng.normal(0.0, step)
            params = np.clip(params, -bound, bound)

        p = basis_make_profile(params, n, B)
        c = conv_full(p, p)
        s = score_from_c(c, p)

        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
            best_score = s
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_params = params.copy()
            step = min(0.55, step * 1.05)
            stall = 0
        else:
            if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score * 1.01 and rng.random() < 0.08:
                # Occasionally accept a near-best perturbation to allow chaining.
                # We still keep best_params unless it is actually better, but this
                # diversifies the next local moves by modifying params in place.
                best_params = 0.7 * best_params + 0.3 * params
                best_params = np.clip(best_params, -bound, bound)
            stall += 1
            step = max(0.02, step * 0.90)

    return best_params, best_p, best_score


def optimize_sequence(p0, deadline, rng, topk=None, step0=0.25, floor=1.0e-9):
    """
    Full-sequence active-constraint multiplicative optimizer.
    """
    p = floor_normalize(np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64), floor)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return None, np.inf

    c = conv_full(p, p)
    score = score_from_c(c, p)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = score

    if topk is None:
        topk = min(32, max(8, n // 16))
    topk = int(max(1, topk))

    step = float(step0)
    no_best = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        # If the best point has not improved for too long, reset and perturb it.
        if no_best > 160:
            sigma = min(0.55, 0.12 + 0.0018 * float(no_best))
            p = block_perturb(best_p, rng, int(rng.integers(2, 5)), sigma)
            c = conv_full(p, p)
            score = score_from_c(c, p)
            if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_p = p.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            no_best = 0
            step = max(0.10, step * 0.55)
            continue

        # If local moves keep failing, inject mild random noise from current point.
        if no_improve > 70:
            p = random_lognoise_update(p, rng, 0.08)
            c = conv_full(p, p)
            score = score_from_c(c, p)
            if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_p = p.copy()
                no_best = 0
            no_improve = 0
            step = max(0.12, step * 0.75)
            continue

        R = active_gradient_from_top(p, c, topk)
        if not np.isfinite(R).all() or not np.any(R > 0.0):
            break

        cand = mirror_update(p, R, step, floor)
        c2 = conv_full(cand, cand)
        s2 = score_from_c(c2, cand)

        if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < score:
            p, c, score = cand, c2, s2
            no_improve = 0
            step = min(1.0, step * 1.03)
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_p = p.copy()
                no_best = 0
            else:
                no_best += 1
        else:
            # Half-step fallback.
            cand2 = mirror_update(p, R, 0.5 * step, floor)
            c3 = conv_full(cand2, cand2)
            s3 = score_from_c(c3, cand2)

            if np.isfinite(s3) and s3 < score:
                p, c, score = cand2, c3, s3
                no_improve = 0
                step = max(0.02, step * 0.85)
                if score < best_score:
                    best_score = score
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    no_best = 0
                else:
                    no_best += 1
            else:
                # Small annealed acceptance to avoid sticking too tightly.
                if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score * 1.003 and rng.random() < 0.10:
                    p, c, score = cand, c2, s2
                step = max(0.02, step * 0.70)
                no_improve += 1
                no_best += 1

    return best_p, best_score


def random_smooth_profile(n, rng):
    """Random low-frequency positive profile."""
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    K = max(3, int(math.sqrt(max(1, n))) // 2)
    for k in range(1, K + 1):
        amp = rng.uniform(0.0, 0.8) / math.sqrt(float(k))
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
        f += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * k * x + phase)

    if rng.random() < 0.7:
        f += rng.uniform(-0.8, 0.8) * (x - 0.5)
    if rng.random() < 0.4:
        f += rng.uniform(0.0, 0.6) * (1.0 - x)
    f += rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n)

    f = np.clip(f, 0.01, None)
    s = f.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return f / s


def structured_block_profile(n, rng):
    """Random disjoint-block profile."""
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    k = int(rng.integers(3, 7))
    lengths = rng.dirichlet(np.full(k, 1.5, dtype=np.float64)) * n
    start = 0

    for L in lengths:
        L = max(2, int(L))
        if start >= n:
            break
        L = min(L, n - start)
        if L <= 0:
            break
        h = rng.uniform(0.2, 2.0)
        p[start:start + L] = h
        gap = int(max(1, n * 0.02 * rng.random()))
        start += L + gap

    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return p / s


def upsample_profile(p, n_to):
    """Linearly interpolate a profile to a new length and renormalize."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    m = int(p.size)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, int(n_to)), dtype=np.float64) / max(1, int(n_to))
    if m == int(n_to):
        return floor_normalize(p, 1.0e-12)
    if m == 1:
        arr = np.full(max(1, int(n_to)), float(p[0]), dtype=np.float64)
        return floor_normalize(arr, 1.0e-12)

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n_to), dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    return floor_normalize(arr, 1.0e-12)


def candidate_key(item):
    return item[0]


def add_candidate(candidates, p, score):
    """Add a candidate to the list and prune poor ones."""
    if p is None:
        return None
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.ndim != 1:
        p = p.ravel()
    if p.size <= 0:
        return None
    if p.size > _MAX_CAND_LEN:
        p = upsample_profile(p, _MAX_CAND_LEN)

    p = floor_normalize(p, 1.0e-10)
    if score is None or not np.isfinite(score) or score <= 0.0:
        score = self_fast_score(p)

    candidates.append((float(score), p.copy()))
    if len(candidates) > 20:
        candidates.sort(key=candidate_key)
        del candidates[12:]
    return p


def run_phase(n, inits, phase_deadline, total_deadline, rng, candidates, topk=None, step0=None):
    """Run several initializations at a fixed length within a time slice."""
    phase_start = time.monotonic()
    if len(inits) <= 0 or phase_deadline <= phase_start:
        return None, np.inf

    phase_deadline = min(phase_deadline, total_deadline)
    per = (phase_deadline - phase_start) / float(len(inits))
    best_p = None
    best_score = np.inf

    for idx, p0 in enumerate(inits):
        seg_deadline = min(phase_start + per * (float(idx) + 1.0), total_deadline)
        if seg_deadline <= time.monotonic():
            s = self_fast_score(p0)
            add_candidate(candidates, p0, s)
            if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
                best_score = s
                best_p = np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            continue

        p, s = optimize_sequence(p0, seg_deadline, rng, topk=topk, step0=step0)
        if p is not None:
            add_candidate(candidates, p, s)
            if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
                best_score = s
                best_p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        else:
            s = self_fast_score(p0)
            add_candidate(candidates, p0, s)
            if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
                best_score = s
                best_p = np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    print(f"phase n={n} best_fast={best_score:.8f}")
    return best_p, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    """Main search entry point."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    reserve = max(12.0, 0.012 * budget)
    search_deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget - reserve)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    candidates = []
    add_candidate(candidates, np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0, 2.0)

    print(f"budget_s={budget}, search_deadline_s={search_deadline - t0:.2f}")

    # Optional parent from previous construction
    parent = None
    parent_obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if parent_obj is not None:
        try:
            parent = np.asarray(parent_obj, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            parent = np.nan_to_num(parent, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if parent.ndim != 1:
                parent = parent.ravel()
            if parent.size > 0 and parent.sum() > 0.0:
                parent = parent / parent.sum()
                add_candidate(candidates, upsample_profile(parent, min(4096, parent.size)), None)
            else:
                parent = None
        except Exception:
            parent = None

    params = None
    if search_deadline - time.monotonic() > 8.0:
        if budget > 800:
            nb, K, basis_slice = 128, 10, 30.0
        elif budget > 300:
            nb, K, basis_slice = 96, 8, 15.0
        else:
            nb, K, basis_slice = 64, 6, 10.0

        basis_deadline = min(time.monotonic() + basis_slice, search_deadline - 3.0)
        try:
            params, p_basis, s_basis = optimize_basis_search(nb, K, basis_deadline, rng)
            if p_basis is not None:
                add_candidate(candidates, p_basis, s_basis)
            print(f"basis score={s_basis:.8f}")
        except Exception as e:
            print("basis search failed:", e)
            params = None

    # Add unoptimized basis profiles at several lengths as cheap diversity.
    if params is not None:
        for n_try in [256, 512, 1024, 2048]:
            if time.monotonic() > search_deadline - 8.0:
                break
            try:
                p_try = make_profile_from_params(params, n_try)
                s_try = self_fast_score(p_try)
                add_candidate(candidates, p_try, s_try)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Choose scales based on available time.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if budget > 800:
        n1, slice1 = 512, 150.0
        n2, slice2 = 2048, 300.0
        n3 = 8192
    elif budget > 300:
        n1, slice1 = 384, 80.0
        n2, slice2 = 1024, 180.0
        n3 = 4096
    else:
        n1, slice1 = 256, 40.0
        n2, slice2 = 512, 80.0
        n3 = 2046

    # Phase 1
    inits1 = []
    if params is not None:
        inits1.append(make_profile_from_params(params, n1))
    inits1.append(random_smooth_profile(n1, rng))
    inits1.append(structured_block_profile(n1, rng))
    if parent is not None:
        inits1.append(upsample_profile(parent, n1))
    inits1 = inits1[:4]

    if time.monotonic() < search_deadline - 3.0:
        p1, s1 = run_phase(n1, inits1, min(time.monotonic() + slice1, search_deadline), search_deadline, rng, candidates)
    else:
        p1, s1 = None, np.inf

    # Phase 2
    inits2 = []
    if p1 is not None:
        inits2.append(p1)
    if params is not None:
        inits2.append(make_profile_from_params(params, n2))
    inits2.append(random_smooth_profile(n2, rng))
    if parent is not None:
        inits2.append(upsample_profile(parent, n2))
    inits2 = inits2[:3]

    if time.monotonic() < search_deadline - 5.0:
        p2, s2 = run_phase(n2, inits2, min(time.monotonic() + slice2, search_deadline), search_deadline, rng, candidates)
    else:
        p2, s2 = p1, s1

    # Phase 3: use remaining search time.
    inits3 = []
    if p2 is not None:
        inits3.append(p2)
    if params is not None:
        inits3.append(make_profile_from_params(params, n3))
    if parent is not None:
        inits3.append(upsample_profile(parent, n3))
    if len(inits3) == 0:
        inits3.append(random_smooth_profile(n3, rng))
    inits3 = inits3[:2]

    if time.monotonic() < search_deadline - 2.0:
        run_phase(n3, inits3, min(time.monotonic() + max(20.0, 300.0), search_deadline), search_deadline, rng, candidates)
    else:
        # Not much time left; just score the current best inits.
        for p0 in inits3:
            add_candidate(candidates, p0, None)

    # Final exact evaluation of top candidates.
    candidates.sort(key=candidate_key)
    top = candidates[:8]

    best_seq = None
    best_exact = np.inf

    for _, p in top:
        try:
            seq = [float(x) for x in p]
            exc = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
        except Exception:
            exc = np.inf
        print(f"final candidate len={len(p)} exact={exc:.8f}")
        if np.isfinite(exc) and exc < best_exact:
            best_exact = exc
            best_seq = seq

    if best_seq is None:
        fallback = [float(x) for x in (np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0)]
        try:
            best_exact = float(evaluate_sequence(fallback))
        except Exception:
            best_exact = 2.0
        best_seq = fallback

    print(f"best_exact={best_exact:.8f}")
    return best_seq
```
